     142 
 143     private fun estimateSpeed(location: Location): Float {
 144         val prev = lastLocation ?: return 0f
 145         val dt = (location.time - prev.time) / 1000f
 146         if (dt <= 0f) return 0f
 147         return try { prev.distanceTo(location) / dt } catch (e: Exception) { 0f }
 148     }

  • C:/Users/vdako/neko-gps/native-android/app/src/main/java/com/nekogps/app/features/routing/AlternativeRoutes.kt:100:33This expression contains a magic number. Consider defining it to a well named constant. 
      97     private fun analyzeSegment(p1: GeoPoint, p2: GeoPoint): SegmentInfo {
  98         val latDiff = abs(p2.latitude - p1.latitude); val lonDiff = abs(p2.longitude - p1.longitude)
  99         val avgLat = (p1.latitude + p2.latitude) / 2.0; val avgLon = (p1.longitude + p2.longitude) / 2.0
 100         val hasToll = (avgLat * 1000).toInt() % 3 == 0 && latDiff > 0.01
 101         val hasHighway = (avgLon * 1000).toInt() % 5 == 0 && lonDiff > 0.02
 102         val hasFerry = (avgLat * 1000).toInt() % 7 == 0 && latDiff < 0.005 && lonDiff < 0.005
 103         return SegmentInfo(hasToll, hasHighway, hasFerry)
  • C:/Users/vdako/neko-gps/native-android/app/src/main/java/com/nekogps/app/features/routing/AlternativeRoutes.kt:100:49This expression contains a magic number. Consider defining it to a well named constant. 
      97     private fun analyzeSegment(p1: GeoPoint, p2: GeoPoint): SegmentInfo {
  98         val latDiff = abs(p2.latitude - p1.latitude); val lonDiff = abs(p2.longitude - p1.longitude)
  99         val avgLat = (p1.latitude + p2.latitude) / 2.0; val avgLon = (p1.longitude + p2.longitude) / 2.0
 100         val hasToll = (avgLat * 1000).toInt() % 3 == 0 && latDiff > 0.01
 101         val hasHighway = (avgLon * 1000).toInt() % 5 == 0 && lonDiff > 0.02
 102         val hasFerry = (avgLat * 1000).toInt() % 7 == 0 && latDiff < 0.005 && lonDiff < 0.005
 103         return SegmentInfo(hasToll, hasHighway, hasFerry)

      99     fun calculateMidwayWaypoint(origin: GeoPoint, destination: GeoPoint, strategy: MidwayStrategy = MidwayStrategy.MIDPOINT): GeoPoint {
 100         return when (strategy) {
 101             MidwayStrategy.MIDPOINT -> GeoPoint((origin.latitude + destination.latitude) / 2.0, (origin.longitude + destination.longitude) / 2.0)
 102             MidwayStrategy.SHORTER_RETURN -> GeoPoint(destination.latitude + (origin.latitude - destination.latitude) * 0.4, destination.longitude + (origin.longitude - destination.longitude) * 0.4)
 103             MidwayStrategy.SCENIC -> { val midLat = (origin.latitude + destination.latitude) / 2.0; val midLon = (origin.longitude + destination.longitude) / 2.0; GeoPoint(midLat + 0.01, midLon + 0.01) }
 104         }
 105     }

     228      * Convert lat/lon to tile numbers at a given zoom level.
 229      */
 230     private fun getTileNumber(lat: Double, lon: Double, zoom: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
 231         val x = ((lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * (1 shl zoom)).toInt()
 232         val y = ((1.0 - Math.log(Math.tan(Math.toRadians(lat)) + 1.0 / Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat))) / Math.PI) / 2.0 * (1 shl zoom)).toInt()
 233         return Pair(x, y)
 234     }
  • C:/Users/vdako/neko-gps/native-android/app/src/main/java/com/nekogps/app/offline/OfflineMapManager.kt:231:34This expression contains a magic number. Consider defining it to a well named constant. 
     228      * Convert lat/lon to tile numbers at a given zoom level.

 230     private fun getTileNumber(lat: Double, lon: Double, zoom: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
 231         val x = ((lon + 180.0) / 360.0 * (1 shl zoom)).toInt()
 232         val y = ((1.0 - Math.log(Math.tan(Math.toRadians(lat)) + 1.0 / Math.cos(Math.toRadians(lat))) / Math.PI) / 2.0 * (1 shl zoom)).toInt()
 233         return Pair(x, y)

  • C:/Users/vdako/neko-gps/native-android/app/src/main/java/com/nekogps/app/utils/CoordinateConverter.kt:171:66This expression contains a magic number. Consider defining it to a well named constant. 
     168      */
 169     fun utmToDecimal(utm: UTM): Pair<Double, Double> {
 170         val easting = utm.easting - 500000.0
 171         val northing = if (utm.hemisphere == "S") utm.northing - 10_000_000.0 else utm.northing
